description: Fisherman's Corner in Perdido Key Florida is a favorite of locals and tourists alike. Top ranked among thousands on Trip Advisor, Urbanspoon, PNJ's "Best Of The Bay" and numerous other entities involved in the culinary community. We have worked hard at putting our humble establishment at the top of the list and we couldn't have accomplished this without our dedicated and much appreciated chefs and loyal customers. Fisherman's Corner has grown from a quaint little gem under the Theo Baars bridge in Perdido Key to a household name that travels well outside Pensacola and the Gulf Coast. Thanks to all of our fans throughout the southeastern US for spreading the word. We hope to be in your hometown soon so you can enjoy our cuisine any day of the week. Fishermans Corner offers a casual fine dining experience with a Creole flair! Come out for a culinary trip! We've put the finest ingredients, freshest fish and seafood, and best prime beef in the hands of the best chefs in town. Add a funky casual atmosphere, friendly staff and a world class wine list - and you have got a night at Fisherman's Corner. Our philosophy is simple: Select only the freshest and highest quality ingredients and put them in the hands of the Gulf Coasts' most talented master chefs. Treat every table to the best dining experience possible with friendly, professional and engaging customer service. Make it a place where everyone fits in with fair prices and full plates, mix it together in a classic funky casual New Orleans atmosphere and the result can only be a jaw dropping culinary experience.
